# 07 Vocabulary, Synonyms, Antonyms, Idioms & Verbal Analogies Master Compendium
**Domain**: Section 3 Verbal Ability & Lexical Competence  
**Target Examination**: Infosys System Engineer (SE) & Specialist Programmer (SP/DSE)  
**Total Consolidated Sources**: 4 Primary Vaults (CampusMonk, OnlineStudy4U, Topmate Paid Content, College ITM)  
**Standard**: 100% Comprehensive | Full Definitions | Etymology & Context | Zero Placeholders  

---

## The Strategic Dimension of Vocabulary in Infosys Recruitment
Lexical items constitute 25% to 30% of Section 3 in the Infosys System Engineer online test. The questions probe not merely surface definitions, but precise contextual shades, idiomatic appropriateness, and relational proportionality:
1. **Direct Synonyms & Antonyms**: Testing high-frequency academic and corporate vocabulary (*aberration, ephemeral, obfuscate, pragmatic, taciturn, ubiquitous*).
2. **One-Word Substitutions**: Specialized lexical terms denoting technical professions, administrative practices, cognitive states, and character traits (*polyglot, altruist, circumlocution, idiosyncrasy*).
3. **Idioms & Phrasal Collocations**: High-frequency corporate and colloquial idioms tested in sentence contexts (*burn the candle at both ends, break the ice, cold shoulder, red herring, white elephant*).
4. **Verbal Analogies (Relational Logic)**: Proportional reasoning between word pairs (*Synonymous, Antonymous, Part-to-Whole, Cause-and-Effect, Worker-and-Tool, Intensity / Degree*):
   $$\text{Word } A : \text{Word } B :: \text{Word } C : \text{Word } D$$

Below is the verified, forensic compilation of all authentic vocabulary, idiom, and analogy questions extracted across all four preparation repositories.
